For a private class with a NULL owner, store the class with owner NULL and let the reader admit admins only, or fall back to class NULL. Never let the CHECK make capture fail.
- **T-16 [minor]** Some later-table foreign keys are id-only where D9 promises composite same-workspace keys. moth_tags.canonical_id REFERENCES moth_tags(id), and moth_tag_nominations.tag_id and result_tag_id REFERENCES moth_tags, carry no workspace. Foreign-key checks bypass RLS, so a cross-tenant id is accepted, or answers 23503 when absent. D10 also cites 0096 as id-only typed foreign keys, but 0096's are composite (id, project_id); the deviation is deliberate and should be stated as such. moth_views is listed under both the populate trigger (D9) and DEFAULT current_workspace_id() (its schema).
  - *Scenario:* A company-tier admin in workspace A sets canonical_id to a tag id from workspace B, through the column grant or a buggy merge RPC. The row commits, linking two tenants, and the 23503-or-success outcome reveals whether that id exists.
  - *Evidence:* Design schema for moth_tags (canonical_id), moth_tag_nominations (tag_id, result_tag_id), D9 ('a composite foreign key to (id, workspace_id)'), D10 ('MEASURED: task_items (0096) already does this'); 0096 task_items_*_fk FOREIGN KEY (x, project_id) REFERENCES … (id, project_id).
  - *Fix:* Make every intra-Moth reference composite on (id, workspace_id) (the UNIQUE (id, workspace_id) already planned on moth_tags), keep canonical_id out of client column grants, and reword D10 as 'unlike 0096, id-only on purpose because files move projects'. Pick one workspace-stamping rule for moth_views.

## 6. Privacy repairs

**Area as the designer framed it:** Privacy repairs Session 1 must make before Moth relies on project visibility: the write-side project hops (A-R2-03, C-R2-01 and the wider class), the money gate's and edit history's missing privacy arm (S4d-01, S4d-02), the owner column private visibility hangs on, and private and personal suppression on the workspace realtime channel (A-R1-04), with a leak-free invalidation for a project made private after the fact.

### Summary

Session 1 should fix the whole measured class, in three migrations that land before any Moth table: write hops, then money and history, then the channel. The class is wider than OUTSTANDING says. I MEASURED this from the latest policy text. Besides tasks and assets, the following have no project hop on at least one write clause: comments, folders, scenes, shots, levels, experiences, project_members, task_templates, milestones (UPDATE USING and DELETE, which 0082 §3b left open), files (UPDATE USING and DELETE, latest 0092) and projects_update itself. project_members_select (0020) and task_templates_select (0044) also leak a private project's roster and pinned templates on read. Separately, fn_audit_touch (0001) fills created_by only when it is NULL and never protects it on UPDATE. Private visibility hangs on created_by, so a writer can claim or privatise a project. A filter-less UPDATE projects SET created_by = me would take over every private project, which is INFERRED and needs a probe. Migration A puts the 0082 §3b hop on every write clause, not just WITH CHECK. A filter-less column-free UPDATE ... SET project_id = <public> moves rows out of a private project through USING, and a filter-less DELETE removes them. Migration A also gives projects_update the privacy arm, adds an owner guard trigger, and closes with a catalogue scan (every gate-calling policy hops) rather than a list. Migration B puts passes_project_privacy inside can_access_project_money. That one change closes S4d-01 for the 8 money storage policies, the 5 budget tables, and every definer and history money arm. Migration B also restates fn_edit_history_capture (0061) so every row carries project_id from one resolver, backfills, and gives edit_history_select a fail-closed privacy arm. Migration C restates fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast (0018). It judges each side of a change (OLD/NEW) separately and never carries a private project's row. When one side is hidden, the event is downgraded to INSERT or DELETE. A change in a project's audience sends a payload-free AUDIENCE signal and no row. On that signal the client refetches the project index through RLS and closes the open project if it is gone. Personal spaces are covered by keeping is_private = true on their backing project, a decision that crosses into another area. Every channel behaviour probe is vacuous on CI and bites on wilson-dev. Body-structure probes bite on CI.

### Decisions

- **D1** Session 1 fixes the whole measured class (every policy listed in schema_changes, the owner column, the money gate, edit history and the workspace broadcast). Only two items go to OUTSTANDING: the Realtime join-cache residual, which the database cannot fix, and a build-time re-measure of SECURITY DEFINER bodies that call a write gate without passes_project_privacy (if any are found unfixed).
  - *Why:* Plan rule: security defects are fixed. Moth's catalogue, glow, counts, trash and boards all assume that project visibility is a boundary, and a write that lands in a private project, or a row that rides the workspace topic, breaks the ruling that nothing appears in Moth that a person cannot already see.
  - *Rejected:* Fixing only A-R2-03's four policies and naming the rest in OUTSTANDING: it leaves 30+ clauses of the same shape open, including a filter-less move out of a private project and a filter-less takeover of the owner column.
- **D2** The project hop goes on EVERY write clause (INSERT WITH CHECK, UPDATE USING and WITH CHECK, DELETE USING) of every project-scoped policy that lacks one. It is the 0082 §3b one-liner `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = <t>.project_id)`, evaluated under the caller's projects_select. For comments the hop resolves through `public.fn_comment_project_id(comments.entity_type, comments.entity_id)`, which hops to the PROJECT, not the entity (the S23 trap). For task_templates it is `project_id IS NULL OR EXISTS(...)`.
  - *Why:* WITH CHECK alone (0082 §3b, 0083) stops moves INTO a private project and in-place rewrites. It does not stop a filter-less, column-free `UPDATE t SET project_id = '<public>'`. Only UPDATE USING applies to that statement and USING has no hop, so a second manager moves a private project's rows into a public one and reads them. The same applies to a filter-less DELETE (C-R2-01). INFERRED from Postgres policy semantics, as C-R2-01 measured for files; pinned by suite 110 probes.
  - *Rejected:* (a) Putting passes_project_privacy inside can_write_project, can_comment_project and can_manage_project_roster: one place, and it would reach definers. But it breaks the house shape every recent table uses (the hop under caller RLS, VW1c rule), leaves writes into trashed projects open, and silently changes the lockstep helpers the client mirrors. (b) WITH CHECK only, as OUTSTANDING proposed: it leaves the move-out and delete shapes open.
- **D3** The post-condition and one suite probe scan the catalogue instead of checking a list. Every public policy whose expression calls can_write_project, can_comment_project, can_manage_project_roster, can_edit_shot_lists or can_write_task_template must carry 'FROM projects' on every non-null clause, unless it is on a short, pinned exception list. The exceptions are asset_versions_*, task_deps_* and task_links_* (they hop through assets or tasks, whose SELECT policies hop to projects) and projects_update (inline privacy arm). Every public table with a project_id column must also have a SELECT policy carrying 'FROM projects' or an allow-listed gate: edit_history and file_events use can_read_project_topic, the budget tables the money gate, upload_reservations its own rows.
  - *Why:* The S22 lesson is to scan every object rather than the list. Moth will add a dozen project-scoped tables, and a forgotten hop on any of them fails CI by name.
  - *Rejected:* A per-policy list in the post-condition: it is exactly what would miss the next table.
- **D4** projects_update USING gains 0072's privacy arm inline: `AND (NOT is_private OR created_by = auth.uid() OR public.current_app_role() = 'admin')`. A new BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE trigger trg_projects_owner_guard (fn_projects_owner_guard, SECURITY INVOKER) binds only when current_user = 'authenticated'. On INSERT it sets NEW.created_by := auth.uid(). On UPDATE it sets NEW.created_by := OLD.created_by, and it refuses a change of is_private unless the caller is OLD.created_by or a workspace admin (42501).
  - *Why:* MEASURED: fn_audit_touch (0001) stamps created_by only when it is NULL and leaves it alone on UPDATE, and no projects trigger guards it, so 0072's header claim that no client can name somebody else as owner is false. Private visibility (projects_select, passes_project_privacy) hangs on created_by. MEASURED: no client writes projects.created_by (PROJECT_COLUMNS, PROJECT_LEFT_AT_HOME), and only ProjectsPage's create path sets is_private, so no production caller changes. 0072 ('private projects for themselves') settles that only the creator or an admin changes who sees a project. Binding only on current_user = 'authenticated' leaves definer RPCs (such as personal-space provisioning) and suite runners alone.
  - *Rejected:* auth.uid() IS NOT NULL as the binding test (0086's idiom): suites that insert as the runner while a persona's claims are still set would change meaning. A WITH CHECK privacy arm on projects_update: it cannot see OLD, so it cannot express 'unchanged owner'.
- **D5** S4d-01 is closed by restating can_access_project_money (0037's body) with one added conjunct, `AND public.passes_project_privacy(p_project)`. The eight money storage policies and the five budget tables keep their text. Suite 95 probe 167 is retargeted to the new whole body.
  - *Why:* The money gate is called from storage.objects policies, 20 budget-table clauses, files and file_events money arms, edit_history_select, can_access_project_legal's money half and definer bodies such as reserve_upload_bytes. A policy hop reaches none of the definers. The only narrowing is for a non-creator, non-admin manager seat on a private project, which the ruling 'private projects are visible only to creator and admins' already excludes; that supplies the nod OUTSTANDING asked for. The admin arm always passes. It also closes S4d review finding 9, the Legal reach through the money half.
  - *Rejected:* Hops in all 8 storage policies plus 20 budget clauses: 28 restatements, the definer callers left open, and suite 95 probe 15's whole-text pins would move as well.
- **D6** S4d-02: fn_edit_history_capture (latest 0061) is restated to stamp edit_history.project_id for every entity type through one new resolver, public.edit_history_project_of(p_entity_type text, p_entity_id uuid, p_row jsonb), which is SECURITY DEFINER and closed to clients. Existing rows are backfilled with the same resolver. edit_history_select (0092) gains `AND (entity_type IN ('rate_cards','rate_card_entries') OR public.current_app_role() = 'admin' OR public.can_read_project_topic(project_id))`, so a NULL project fails closed to admins.
  - *Why:* Capture holds the full row (OLD on delete), so a delete resolves its project even when a cascade has removed the parent, and fails closed when it cannot. can_read_project_topic is the SECURITY INVOKER gate file_events_select already uses (privacy and live project under the caller's RLS), with the same admin arm for history that outlives its project. fn_edit_history_money_snapshot still overwrites project_id for files, unchanged.
  - *Rejected:* (a) A second BEFORE INSERT trigger on edit_history: suite 95 probe 171 pins exactly one trigger, and diffs of updates lack the row. (b) passes_project_privacy in the policy: it is REVOKEd from authenticated, and granting it would create an oracle for private ids. (c) A hop with project_id IS NULL readable: it leaks cascaded deletes.
- **D7** The workspace broadcast is restated from 0018, keeping every existing filter word for word, and it judges each side of a change separately. For projects a side is visible when is_private is false. For tasks, assets and project_members a side is visible when its project row exists with NOT is_private, in 0101's shape. workspace_members are unchanged. When both sides are visible the event is sent as before. When only NEW is visible it goes out INSERT-shaped (NEW, NULL), and when only OLD is visible DELETE-shaped (NULL, OLD), so a hidden side's values never ride. When neither side is visible nothing is sent. A projects UPDATE whose visibility changes sends no row: it sends `realtime.send({table:'projects', schema:'public', operation:'AUDIENCE', record:null, old_record:null}, 'AUDIENCE', 'rabbit:workspace:<ws>', true)` in both directions.
  - *Why:* broadcast_changes has no per-recipient filter (S4b-03), and the workspace join gate is membership only. A payload-free signal discloses only that some project's audience changed. Clients then learn the truth through RLS: the creator and admins keep the project and everyone else drops it.
  - *Rejected:* (a) OUTSTANDING's candidate (skip private rows, send nothing on the flip): the name stays in others' indexes until their next refetch. (b) Broadcasting the flipped row: it carries the row that must not be carried. (c) An id-only or DELETE-shaped flip event: it names the project, and the creator's and admins' own windows would drop a project they can still see.
- **D8** Client changes. The adapter binds the 'AUDIENCE' broadcast event on the workspace channel. RabbitProvider.handleWorkspaceEvent handles a projects AUDIENCE event by refetching the index through RLS (debounced). If the open project is no longer listed, it closes it through the existing project-trashed teardown and shows a short plain notice. A projects row arriving with is_private === true on the workspace channel is never merged; it schedules the same refetch, as a defence against a server that is behind. realtimeMerge emits a new 'audience' effect when the project topic delivers a projects UPDATE flipping is_private to true, and the provider handles it the same way. useMyTasks needs no change (MEASURED: every projects event is already relevant there).
  - *Why:* Clients cannot know entitlement locally, and RLS is the boundary. The project-topic arm covers a teammate who has the project open at the moment of the flip, even while the workspace channel is down.
  - *Rejected:* Dropping the id locally on AUDIENCE: it hides the project from its creator and admins until the refetch.
- **D9** A personal space's backing project carries is_private = true, and its own classifier (owned by the schema area) handles upload routing and Moth owner-only rules. Every privacy mechanism here then covers it with no extra arm: projects_select, passes_project_privacy, both workspace broadcasts, every hop and the money gate.
  - *Why:* One privacy flag keeps the suppression in one place. The routing conflict the digest measured (uploadFile sends every non-money upload of an is_private project to LOCAL_SERVER) is fixed in uploadFile by testing the personal classifier first, not by dropping is_private. If the schema area rejects this, migration C must name its classifier, land after it, and restate fn_task_items_ws_broadcast (0101) too; suite 104 probe ~16 then moves.
  - *Rejected:* A separate personal flag with its own arm in every gate: five more restatements, and a missed one leaks.
- **D10** There are three migrations, each with its own suite in the 82/83 precedent shape and none covering a new table: A 0107_private_project_write_hops.sql with 110_private_project_write_hops.sql; B 0108_private_project_money_and_history.sql with 111_private_project_money_and_history.sql; C 0109_private_workspace_channel.sql with 112_private_workspace_channel.sql. All three are registered in rls.yml's replay list and rlsSuiteLists.test.js's tail pins, and all land before any Moth table migration (remeasure the numbers at build).
  - *Why:* Moth depends on all three. Separate files keep each rehearsal and planted control small, and OUTSTANDING put A-R2-03 and C-R2-01 in one migration and the broadcast in its own.
  - *Rejected:* Extending 03_assets and 04_tasks (the plan's wording): the repair spans about 15 tables, and per-table edits would renumber many plans.
- **D11** project_members_select (0020) and task_templates_select (0044) gain the hop. Rosters and pinned templates of a private project become invisible to non-viewers.
  - *Why:* MEASURED: both are workspace-plus-membership only, so a private project's id, roster and template names reach every member through a plain SELECT. That is the same leak A-R1-04 fixes on the channel.
  - *Rejected:* Leaving reads alone because the task named only writes: the ruling forbids revealing existence.

### Schema changes

- **public.tasks policies tasks_insert / tasks_update / tasks_delete (migration A)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql): DROP + CREATE each from 0013's body word for word, appending `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = tasks.project_id)` to tasks_insert WITH CHECK, to tasks_update USING and WITH CHECK, and to tasks_delete USING. tasks_select (0097) is untouched.
- **public.assets policies assets_insert / assets_update / assets_delete (A)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql): As tasks: 0013 bodies plus `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = assets.project_id)` on all four clauses.
- **public.comments policies comments_insert / comments_update / comments_delete (A)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql): 0013 bodies plus `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = public.fn_comment_project_id(comments.entity_type, comments.entity_id))` on every clause. entity_type CHECK (0000) is project|phase|asset|task, and all four resolve.
- **public.folders policies folders_insert / folders_update / folders_delete (A)** (restates from: 0041_folders.sql): 0041 bodies plus the folders.project_id hop on every clause.
- **public.scenes, shots, levels, experiences policies <t>_insert / <t>_update / <t>_delete (A)** (restates from: 0040_scenes_shots_levels_experiences.sql): 0040 bodies plus the <t>.project_id hop on every clause (12 policies, 16 clauses).
- **public.project_members policies (A)** (restates from: 0020_admin_grants_and_alignment.sql (select); 0013_project_members.sql (writes)): project_members_select: 0020 body (workspace + has_active_membership) plus `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = project_members.project_id)`. _insert / _update (USING and WITH CHECK) / _delete: 0013 bodies plus the same hop. No recursion: projects_select calls only definer helpers.
- **public.files policies files_update / files_delete (A)** (restates from: 0092_legal_gate_managers.sql): 0092 bodies word for word (money and Legal arms). files_update USING gains `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = files.project_id)`, and its WITH CHECK already has it (0083). files_delete USING gains the same hop (C-R2-01). files_select and files_insert are untouched.
- **public.milestones policies milestones_update (USING) / milestones_delete (A)** (restates from: 0082_private_project_definers.sql (update); 0067_milestones.sql (delete)): milestones_update: 0082 §3b body, with the USING clause gaining `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = milestones.project_id)`. milestones_delete: 0067 body plus the same hop.
- **public.task_templates policies select / insert / update / delete (A)** (restates from: 0044_task_templates.sql): 0044 bodies plus `AND (project_id IS NULL OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = task_templates.project_id))` on every clause, so global templates are unchanged.
- **public.projects policy projects_update (A)** (restates from: 0013_project_members.sql): 0013 body. USING gains `AND (NOT is_private OR created_by = auth.uid() OR public.current_app_role() = 'admin')`. WITH CHECK is unchanged (workspace), because the owner guard below handles the owner column and the flip.
- **public.fn_projects_owner_guard() + trigger trg_projects_owner_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.projects FOR EACH ROW (A)** (restates from: new): plpgsql, SECURITY INVOKER, SET search_path = public, REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated. Body: `IF current_user <> 'authenticated' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF; IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN NEW.created_by := auth.uid(); RETURN NEW; END IF; NEW.created_by := OLD.created_by; IF NEW.is_private IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.is_private AND OLD.created_by IS DISTINCT FROM auth.uid() AND COALESCE(public.current_app_role(),'') <> 'admin' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'only the person who made a project, or an admin, can change who sees it' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF; RETURN NEW;`. COMMENT ON COLUMN projects.is_private and created_by are restated to say who may change them. Post-conditions: enabled BEFORE row trigger for INSERT and UPDATE, no client EXECUTE, and the comment-stripped body names OLD.created_by and is_private.
- **Migration A post-conditions and pre-flight** (restates from: new): Pre-flight: 0072 (projects.is_private, projects_select reads is_private), 0082 (passes_project_privacy) and 0092 (files_delete carries can_access_project_legal) are present, or RAISE. Post-conditions on pg_policies (deparsed, by name): every restated clause keeps its prior gates (current_workspace_id, has_active_membership where it had it, its write gate, files' file_row_is_money / can_access_project_legal arms) AND names 'projects'. Each touched table keeps exactly its permissive policy names (no policy added beside). The catalogue scan of D3 finds no unhopped gate-calling clause outside the pinned exception list. The owner trigger exists.
- **public.can_access_project_money(uuid) (B)** (restates from: 0037_budget_system.sql): CREATE OR REPLACE, same signature, grants unchanged: `SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id = p_project AND p.workspace_id = public.current_workspace_id() AND public.has_active_membership(p.workspace_id)) AND public.passes_project_privacy(p_project) AND (public.current_app_role() = 'admin' OR public.project_role_for(p_project) = 'manager');` with LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public. The COMMENT is restated. Post-conditions: the whole body is pinned (whitespace collapsed), passes_project_privacy appears exactly once, prosecdef / stable / search_path hold, and can_access_project_legal's body is still 0092's whole.
- **public.edit_history_project_of(p_entity_type text, p_entity_id uuid, p_row jsonb) RETURNS uuid (B)** (restates from: new): plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public, REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, GRANT service_role. Refuses p_entity_type outside the 14-name allowlist (0061's edit_history_entity_type_check). If p_row lacks the needed key, it reads the live row with `EXECUTE format('SELECT to_jsonb(t) FROM public.%I t WHERE id = $1', p_entity_type)`. Mapping: projects→id; phases, assets, tasks, files, ingestion_runs→project_id; comments→fn_comment_project_id(entity_type, entity_id); asset_versions→assets.project_id via asset_id; task_dependencies→tasks via predecessor_id; phase_dependencies→phases via predecessor_id; task_links→tasks via task_id; ingestion_chunks→ingestion_runs via run_id; rate_cards and rate_card_entries→NULL. Any error returns NULL.
- **public.fn_edit_history_capture() (B)** (restates from: 0061_phase_dependencies.sql): 0061's body word for word, plus `v_project := public.edit_history_project_of(TG_TABLE_NAME, (v_row->>'id')::uuid, v_row);` after step (d), and project_id added to the INSERT column and VALUES lists. EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS → WARNING is kept, and grants are re-applied. Post-conditions on the comment-stripped body: all eight parent-join table names, the resolver called once, 'project_id' in the INSERT, the EXCEPTION guard, SECURITY DEFINER with search_path; the 14 trg_<t>_edit_history triggers still run it.
- **public.edit_history backfill and comment (B)** (restates from: 0088_legal_files.sql (column comment)): `UPDATE public.edit_history SET project_id = public.edit_history_project_of(entity_type, entity_id, CASE action WHEN 'update' THEN NULL ELSE COALESCE(diff->'new', diff->'old') END) WHERE project_id IS NULL AND entity_type NOT IN ('rate_cards','rate_card_entries');` (monotone: it only fills NULLs). COMMENT ON COLUMN edit_history.project_id is restated (0088 said 'files rows only').
- **public.edit_history policy edit_history_select (B)** (restates from: 0092_legal_gate_managers.sql): 0092's body word for word plus `AND (entity_type IN ('rate_cards', 'rate_card_entries') OR public.current_app_role() = 'admin' OR public.can_read_project_topic(project_id))`, with the COMMENT restated. Post-condition: the qual names the money arm, the Legal arm, can_read_project_topic and 'rate_cards'.
- **public.fn_workspace_realtime_broadcast() (C)** (restates from: 0018_workspace_channel.sql): 0018's body restated. It keeps the catalog probe, the tasks assignee/reviewer filter word for word, the assets deleted_at/name/phase_id filter word for word, the workspace_id read and the EXCEPTION guard. Sketch: `v_new := to_jsonb(NEW); v_old := to_jsonb(OLD); IF TG_TABLE_NAME='workspace_members' THEN v_new_ok := v_new IS NOT NULL; v_old_ok := v_old IS NOT NULL; ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='projects' THEN v_new_ok := v_new IS NOT NULL AND (v_new->>'is_private')::boolean IS FALSE; v_old_ok := v_old IS NOT NULL AND (v_old->>'is_private')::boolean IS FALSE; IF TG_OP='UPDATE' AND v_new_ok IS DISTINCT FROM v_old_ok THEN IF EXISTS(realtime.send in pg_proc) THEN PERFORM realtime.send(jsonb_build_object('table','projects','schema','public','operation','AUDIENCE','record',NULL,'old_record',NULL),'AUDIENCE','rabbit:workspace:'||v_ws::text,true); END IF; RETURN NULL; END IF; ELSE v_new_ok := v_new IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.projects p WHERE p.id=(v_new->>'project_id')::uuid AND NOT p.is_private); v_old_ok := <same on v_old>; END IF; IF v_new_ok AND v_old_ok THEN broadcast_changes(topic,TG_OP,TG_OP,TG_TABLE_NAME,TG_TABLE_SCHEMA,NEW,OLD) ELSIF v_new_ok THEN broadcast_changes(topic,'INSERT','INSERT',…,NEW,NULL) ELSIF v_old_ok THEN broadcast_changes(topic,'DELETE','DELETE',…,NULL,OLD) END IF;`. If the rehearsal shows broadcast_changes refusing a bare NULL record, the downgrade arms send 0101's thin-event shape through realtime.send, with record or old_record set to the one visible side's jsonb. The five triggers are unchanged. The COMMENT is restated, and so is can_read_workspace_topic's COMMENT, because 'Membership ≡ visibility' has been false since 0072.
- **Migration C post-conditions** (restates from: new): The comment-stripped body names is_private at least 4 times, 'AUDIENCE' once, 'assignee_id', 'reviewer_id', 'phase_id', 'rabbit:workspace:' and 'EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS'. The realtime.send payload is built from literals only (no NEW, OLD, v_new or v_old inside it). prosecdef with search_path=public and no client EXECUTE. The five trg_<t>_ws_realtime triggers exist, enabled, AFTER I/U/D. budget_versions still has no whole-row broadcast trigger (0101's money rule restated). fn_task_items_ws_broadcast still names is_private (0101).

The `!p.table` guard stays. subscribeProjectChanges is unchanged. *(production caller: RabbitProvider's workspace-channel effect (the existing caller of subscribeWorkspaceChanges); no new export.)*

If the open project's id is absent, push a {type:'project-trashed', id} effect so the existing single teardown path runs (setActiveProjectIdState(null), EMPTY_BUNDLE, clearHistory), and show a plain notice through the provider's existing notice/error surface (copy along the lines of 'This project is no longer shared with you.'). (2) A projects row with is_private === true arriving on the workspace channel is not merged; schedule the same refetch. (3) Handle the new 'audience' effect from realtimeMerge the same way as (1).
